## Standing operator directives (cross-phase)

- **2026-07-22 — Four-stage packet pipeline (quality-first; ratified in chat; supersedes single-agent packet execution from P3-B4-2 onward).** Every packet runs grounding → executor → evaluation → finalizing per `.claude/skills/p3-implementation/SKILL.md` §Work packets: fresh-context stages, all coordinator-launched; `P3/briefs/` holds the handoff artifact which doubles as the evaluation rubric; executor is `opus` ALWAYS (judge ≥ executor AND judge ≠ executor per the D3-ratified research; classifier immunity mid-run); grounding/evaluation Fable — all-`opus` on S10/S11-dense packets and lossless `opus` fallback on any classifier trip; findings drain [F1..Fn] round-capped at 2. Research basis: Anthropic's three-agent harness (planning/generation/evaluation + structured handoff artifacts; InfoQ 2026-04), official "Prompting Claude Fable 5" (fresh-context verifiers outperform self-critique; benign-cyber false positives → Opus routing), D3 record `P3/gates/B3-report.md` §7. Autonomy contract: the operator starts ONE session ("continue implementation"); it stops only at phase gates, spec conflicts, and operator hands-on items.
- **2026-07-20 — Codor harvest: adopt the copy rows, coherence-verified.** Operator directed (chat): adopt the copy candidates from `Research/18-codor-harvest-addendum.md`, but only after verifying coherence with the architecture. Verification ran same day — **verdicts + binding conditions in Research/18 §7** (the authoritative record). Effect on packet cuts: **B3 cut MUST include C1-recitation** (per-turn delivery of ledger `state`/`next_actions` via a PostToolUse `sinet engine-hook` trigger over the existing S03.4 ctl-dir airlock — NO new socket/credential; first step = the pre-registered ~$0.02 PostToolUse `additionalContext` probe, B1-4 method; consumes ⚙ `context.recitation_interval_turns`; all seven §7-C1 conditions bind) and **C3 conformance rows**; **B5/B6 cuts include C2** (serve-side redaction of observability projections + redact-before-match search; constant-on, never applied to deliverable content); **C5 discretionary** (advisory copy-aside context peek). Explicitly NOT adopted: C1-ext (cancel-notice/operator-note — S00.9 amendment territory), C4 (parked at the S04 ≥50-item fan-out row). Nothing from codor is ever a dependency.
- **2026-07-20 — "Settings tab must show AND allow changing everything."** Operator founding requirement (restated at the B1 gate): every operator-relevant number is visible and editable in the settings surface — including numbers S18 deliberately left as **code constants**: auth numbers (TTL/PIN attempts/lockout — B0 gate D2), scheduler structural constants (lane-concurrency default 1 / poll 500 ms / aging biases — B1 gate §4.4), price-table bits, and any others surfaced. **Deferred by the operator ("fix later") — NOT a B2 blocker.** Resolution path: an S00.9 amendment adding the non-⚙ numbers to S18 as **clamped** ⚙ settings (clamps keep invariants — e.g. aging biases can't break the S10.7 no-starvation guarantee). Natural home: around B6 settings UI (S15.9) or a dedicated pass. **This directive makes the "keep as constants" gate decisions (B0 D2, B1 §4.4) INTERIM, not final.** Also in auto-memory `settings-tab-see-change-everything`.

Model routing (2026-07-22 pipeline directive): every packet's executor+finalizer = **opus**; grounding+evaluation inherit (Fable) — S15/FC-v1 frontend scope is NOT S10/S11 security-dense (HANDOFF §5 calibration note); lossless opus fallback on any classifier trip.
